  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用VLM训练成本高昂，而当前普遍假设创建领域专家模型需要微调越来越大规模的基础模型。作者认为这一假设存在误区，希望在兽医放射学这一垂直领域证明：通过精细的工程化设计，小规模定制模型可以在特定任务上超越大型基础模型，从而降低领域AI开发的门槛和成本。

Method: 作者重新设计了整个VLM流水线，包括：(1)文本分词策略；(2)预训练方法；(3)grounding机制；(4)推理流程。提出了新的生成式预训练和grounding策略，以提升训练效率、数据利用率和下游性能。

Result: 仅使用通用模型的一小部分参数、数据和计算资源，从零训练出了首个能够为兽医X光片生成诊断报告的VLM，在性能上以显著优势超越了开放基础模型。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，在特定垂直领域中，"从零训练+精细工程"的路线可以替代"大规模预训练+微调"的范式，为低成本构建领域专家VLM提供了可行方案。

Abstract: While generalist VLMs are expensive to train, creating domain experts is widely assumed to require fine-tuning increasingly large foundation models. We show that, in veterinary radiology, this assumption is misguided. By rethinking the entire VLM pipeline - from text tokenisation and pre-training to grounding and inference - we demonstrate that careful engineering can yield models that outperform much larger foundation models from scratch, without relying on any other data. Our approach introduces new strategies for generative pre-training and grounding that improve training efficiency, increasing data utilisation and downstream performance. Using only a fraction of the parameters, data, and compute of contemporary generalist models, we develop the first VLM capable of generating diagnostic reports for veterinary radiographs, surpassing open foundation models on this task by significant margin.

</details>


### [46] [HandMvNet: Real-Time 3D Hand Pose Estimation Using Multi-View Cross-Attention Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20093)
*Muhammad Asad Ali,Nadia Robertini,Didier Stricker*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HandMvNet是一种实时多视角3D手部运动与形状估计方法，通过多视角注意力融合机制整合多摄像头特征，无需相机参数输入即可生成一致的绝对手部姿态和形状，在保持竞争力的同时显著降低推理时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目方法存在尺度-深度模糊问题，难以获得一致的绝对手部姿态和形状；现有多视角方法依赖相机参数作为输入来学习3D几何，且推理时间较长，不适用于实时应用场景。

Method: HandMvNet采用多视角注意力融合机制，有效整合来自多个视角的特征信息，从多视角图像中直接学习3D几何，消除了对相机参数输入的需求。

Result: 在公开数据集上的定量和定性评估表明，HandMvNet在相同设置下优于之前的方法，同时大幅减少推理时间，达到实时处理能力，并与最先进方法相比具有竞争力。

Conclusion: HandMvNet是首个用于多视角3D手部运动与形状估计的实时方法，解决了单目方法的尺度模糊问题和多视角方法的效率问题，适用于实际实时应用。

Abstract: In this work, we present HandMvNet, one of the first real-time method designed to estimate 3D hand motion and shape from multi-view camera images. Unlike previous monocular approaches, which suffer from scale-depth ambiguities, our method ensures consistent and accurate absolute hand poses and shapes. This is achieved through a multi-view attention-fusion mechanism that effectively integrates features from multiple viewpoints. In contrast to previous multi-view methods, our approach eliminates the need for camera parameters as input to learn 3D geometry. HandMvNet also achieves a substantial reduction in inference time while delivering competitive results compared to the state-of-the-art methods, making it suitable for real-time applications. Evaluated on publicly available datasets, HandMvNet qualitatively and quantitatively outperforms previous methods under identical settings. Code is available at github.com/pyxploiter/handmvnet.

</details>


### [47] [Structured Affinity for Unsupervised Visual Class-Incremental Memory in Deep Artificial Immune Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20104)
*Siphesihle Sithungu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究基于结构化、无梯度的免疫亲和力构建深度人工免疫网络（Deep AIN），使其无需回放即可实现视觉类增量表示记忆学习。通过将B细胞形式化为结构化模板（移位模板亲和力、ZNCC滤波器、特征图绑定图谱），并将结合图谱响应图传递给后续免疫层获得深度，Deep AIN实现了自适应潜坐标重组。实验表明特征图谱Deep AIN在sklearn digits、MNIST、Fashion-MNIST、KMNIST上无需回放即学习到类判别视觉记忆，sklearn digits达到0.939最终平衡准确率，层间尺度校准后两层的Fashion-MNIST达0.814、KMNIST达0.853。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统视觉人工免疫网络（AIN）依赖扁平化向量亲和力，忽略了空间结构；而AIN天然具有记忆形成能力，但尚未被证明可作为无需回放的视觉类增量表示记忆学习者。作者希望探索结构化、无梯度的免疫亲和力能否使深度AIN在这一任务中发挥作用。

Method: 将视觉B细胞形式化为包含移位模板亲和力、零归一化互相关（ZNCC）滤波器及特征图结合图谱的结构化模板；将免疫库同时作为记忆和表示诱导基；通过将结合图谱响应图传递到后续免疫层获取深度。训练过程中无回放、无标签驱动免疫更新、无通过免疫层的反向传播，仅依赖结构化亲和力和自适应层间尺度标定。

Result: scalar结合图谱变体表现不佳，而特征图谱Deep AIN可学习类判别视觉记忆。sklearn digits上logistic回归探针最终平衡准确率0.939、1-NN为0.902，初始类保留率0.978；自适应层间尺度校准后两层特征图谱Deep AIN达0.978平衡准确率。Fashion-MNIST达0.814、KMNIST达0.853。

Conclusion: 结构化亲和力、响应图保留、自适应潜重组和层间尺度校准是实现无需回放的视觉免疫记忆的关键机制。探针仅用于外部验证而非AIN组成部分。该工作表明深度AIN可作为一种可行的人工免疫类增量记忆学习范式。

Abstract: Artificial immune networks (AINs) are naturally memory-forming systems, but conventional visual AINs often rely on flattened vector affinity that ignores spatial structure. This paper studies whether structured, gradient-free immune affinity can make Deep AINs viable as replay-free visual class-incremental representation-memory learners. Visual B-cells are formalized as structured templates, including shifted-template affinity, zero-normalized cross-correlation (ZNCC) filters, and feature-map binding profiles. A repertoire is treated both as memory and as a representation-inducing basis, while depth is obtained by passing binding-profile response maps to subsequent immune layers. The resulting Deep AIN exhibits adaptive latent coordinate reorganization: as new classes arrive, the binding-profile space evolves while retaining recoverable structure for earlier classes. Experiments on sklearn digits, MNIST, Fashion-MNIST, and KMNIST show that preserving response maps is critical. Scalar binding-profile variants underperform, whereas feature-map Deep AINs learn class-discriminative visual memory without replay, label-driven immune updates, or backpropagation through the immune layers. On sklearn digits, downstream probes fitted on the learned binding profiles reach 0.939 final balanced accuracy with logistic regression and 0.902 with 1-nearest-neighbour after all ten classes are encountered, with initial-class retention of 0.978. Adaptive layer-wise scale calibration further improves the two-layer feature-map Deep AIN to 0.978 balanced accuracy. With the same calibration rule, Fashion-MNIST reaches 0.814 and KMNIST reaches 0.853. These probes are external validation tools, not components of the AIN. The results identify structured affinity, response-map preservation, adaptive latent reorganization, and layer-wise scale calibration as key mechanisms for replay-free visual immune memory.

</details>


### [48] [BeyondMasks: Evaluating Causal and Physical Consistency in Video Object Removal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20107)
*Yigit Ekin,Enes Sanli,Aykut Erdem,Erkut Erdem,Aysegul Dundar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出BeyondMasks基准和CORE评估协议，将视频物体移除从局部修复重新定义为因果场景一致性任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频物体移除评估仅关注掩码区域保真度，将其视为局部修复问题，但真实场景中物体移除是因果干预，需要同时移除物体引发的物理效应（阴影、反射、光照变化、半透明、动态痕迹）。现有基准缺乏对齐的干净参考或局限于简化合成场景，无法系统评估因果一致性。

Method: 构建BeyondMasks成对基准，包含时间对齐的合成与真实世界视频对及干净背景参考，覆盖光照、几何、体积和动态交互；提出CORE结构化视觉语言模型评估协议，联合衡量物体消失和效应一致性。

Result: 对最先进方法的测试揭示了系统性失败：尽管掩码区域保真度很高，但方法在移除次级物理效应方面普遍存在不足，暴露了视觉合理性（visual plausibility）与因果正确性（causal correctness）之间的差距。CORE评估与人类判断的一致性优于现有指标。

Conclusion: BeyondMasks将视频物体移除重新定义为因果场景一致性而非局部重建，提供了一个统一的评估框架来衡量物体移除的因果正确性。

Abstract: Recent advances in generative video models have significantly improved visual realism in video object removal, yet evaluation protocols still focus on masked region fidelity, treating removal as local inpainting. In real scenes, object removal is a causal intervention: eliminating an object also requires removing its induced physical effects, such as shadows, reflections, illumination changes, translucency, and dynamic traces. Existing benchmarks lack aligned clean references or remain limited to simplified synthetic settings, preventing systematic evaluation of causal consistency. We introduce BeyondMasks, a paired benchmark for causally consistent video object removal, consisting of temporally aligned synthetic and real world video pairs with clean background references. The dataset spans diverse photometric, geometric, volumetric, and dynamic interactions, and supports both mask based and instruction driven editing. We further propose CORE, a structured vision language model based evaluation protocol that jointly measures object disappearance and after effect consistency, aligning more closely with human judgments than existing metrics. Benchmarking state of the art methods reveals systematic failures in removing secondary physical effects despite high masked region fidelity, exposing a gap between visual plausibility and causal correctness. BeyondMasks reframes video object removal as causal scene consistency rather than local reconstruction and provides a unified framework for its evaluation.

</details>


### [49] [ArmorOCR: Grounded Adversarial Visual Perception via Observation-Transferred Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20122)
*Linhan Cao,Siyuan Li,Jun Lan,Liangbo He,Guannan Li,Xiaolei Huang,Jun Jia,Shuheng Zhou,Huijia Zhu,Weiqiang Wang,Wei Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AdvSpot——首个用于接地对抗性OCR评估的基准，包含390张带区域级标注的图像，覆盖5个主要类别和13种细粒度对抗性OCR类型；同时提出ArmorOCR两阶段训练框架，通过On-Policy自蒸馏和GRPO强化学习提升LMM的对抗性OCR感知能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型多模态模型（LMMs）虽具备强OCR识别能力，但对人类可读但模型难以定位和识别的对抗性视觉文本仍然脆弱。现有OCR基准主要聚焦自然或文档风格文本，缺乏规模大、任务覆盖广且具备区域感知评估的对抗性OCR基准。

Method: 将对抗性OCR形式化为接地OCR感知任务。提出AdvSpot基准（390张图像，区域级标注，5大类13种子类型）。提出ArmorOCR两阶段训练框架：第一阶段通过On-Policy Self-Distillation（OPSD）从特权变换观测中获取缺失的对抗性OCR感知；第二阶段通过Group Relative Policy Optimization（GRPO）结合任务条件化奖励（覆盖定位、识别、完整定位与VQA）细化接地OCR感知。

Result: 在AdvSpot、其他对抗性OCR基准和通用OCR基准上的实验表明，ArmorOCR在不断提升对抗性OCR感知能力的同时，保持了有竞争力的通用OCR能力。

Conclusion: 通过专用的对抗性OCR基准和两阶段训练框架，可以有效增强LMMs对对抗性视觉文本的稳健感知能力，且不牺牲通用OCR性能。

Abstract: Large multimodal models (LMMs) have demonstrated strong OCR recognition capabilities, yet remain vulnerable to adversarial visual text that is readable to humans but challenging for models to localize and recognize. Existing OCR benchmarks mainly focus on natural or document-style text, while adversarial OCR evaluations remain limited in scale, task coverage, or region-aware evaluation. In this paper, we formulate adversarial OCR as a \textbf{grounded OCR perception} task and introduce \textbf{AdvSpot}, the first benchmark for grounded adversarial OCR evaluation. AdvSpot comprises 390 images with region-level annotations, spanning 5 primary categories and 13 fine-grained adversarial OCR types. To address this challenge, we propose \textbf{ArmorOCR}, a two-stage training framework for robust adversarial OCR perception. ArmorOCR first acquires missing adversarial OCR perception from privileged transformed observations through On-Policy Self-Distillation (OPSD), and then refines grounded OCR perception through Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) with task-conditioned rewards for localization, recognition, full spotting, and visual question answering (VQA). Experiments on our AdvSpot, other adversarial OCR benchmarks, and general OCR benchmarks demonstrate that ArmorOCR consistently improves adversarial OCR perception while preserving competitive general OCR capability.

</details>


### [50] [ID-VTG: Image-Disambiguated Video Temporal Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20127)
*Minghang Zheng,Jingli Wei,Hongyi Yang,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出图像消歧视频时间定位（ID-VTG）任务，利用参考图像+文本的多模态查询来精确定位视频中特定实例执行特定动作的片段，并构建了IDVTG-Gym和IDVTG-InternVid两个基准，提出基于双分支快慢架构的VGD-Agg框架，引入Compare Token和Depress Value两个可学习标记来提高区分度，取得了SOTA结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VTG任务仅依赖文本查询，当视频中包含多个视觉相似实体的事件时，难以仅靠文字准确描述细粒度视觉属性来区分目标实例，导致定位准确率下降。

Method: 提出ID-VTG任务及VGD-Agg框架：(1) 构建两个基准IDVTG-Gym（精细体操动作）和IDVTG-InternVid（开放世界多样实体）；(2) 采用双分支快慢架构，快分支生成初步事件提案，慢分支进行帧与参考图像的细粒度匹配；(3) 引入Compare Token表示硬负样本，用于探测目标实例是否存在；引入Depress Value表示与文本无关的事件，将缺少目标实例的提案推向Depress Value以促进文本消歧。

Result: 在两个提出的基准上取得了最先进的结果。

Conclusion: 通过引入图像作为额外的查询模态，能有效解决文本描述难以捕捉细粒度视觉属性的问题，显著提升视频时间定位在视觉相似实体场景下的性能，代码已开源。

Abstract: Video Temporal Grounding (VTG) faces significant challenges when natural language queries must distinguish between multiple events involving visually similar entities, particularly when relying on fine-grained visual attributes that are difficult to describe accurately in words alone. To address this, we introduce Image-Disambiguated Video Temporal Grounding (ID-VTG), a task that leverages multimodal queries combining a reference image and a text description to precisely localize segments where a specific instance performs a described action. To facilitate research, we construct two benchmarks: IDVTG-Gym, focusing on fine-grained, compositionally ordered gymnastics actions with athletes in similar uniforms; and IDVTG-InternVid, an open-world dataset featuring diverse entities (e.g., humans, animals, fictional characters) and significant temporal distractors. Methodologically, we propose the Visually-Guided Disambiguation Aggregation (VGD-Agg) framework based on a dual-branch fast-slow architecture. The fast branch efficiently generates preliminary event proposals, while the slow branch performs fine-grained frame-level matching between video frames and the reference image. We enhance discriminability via two learnable tokens: a Compare Token, which represents hard negatives to probe for the presence of the target instance (as referred to by the query image), and a Depress Value, which represents text-irrelevant events. Proposals that the Compare Token identifies as lacking the target instance are pushed toward the Depress Value, thus easing disambiguation via the text query. Extensive experiments validate our approach, which achieves state-of-the-art results on the proposed benchmarks. Code is available at https://github.com/oceanflowlab/ID-VTG.

</details>


### [51] [Feature Evolution and Migration during Vision Transformer Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20134)
*Joonas Järve,Halil Ibrahim Aysel,Tarun Khajuria,Meelis Kull*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的视角来理解Vision Transformer（ViT）中的特征演化，通过两个维度——网络深度（层）和训练时间（epoch）——来可视化训练过程。使用稀疏自编码器（SAEs）从CLS token表示中提取候选稀疏特征，并比较不同epoch-层对之间的激活轮廓，从而研究特征级别的动态变化。实验发现了特征迁移现象（特征最可检测的层在训练中发生变化），并表明迁移集中在训练早期、更多朝向前层而非深层方向，且随着特征组织稳定而减少。深层比浅层更早、更强烈地稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的表示级相似度度量无法直接观察到特征级别的动态变化，作者希望理解ViT在训练过程中特征如何演化，并提出特征迁移（feature migration）这一新概念来刻画特征在层间移动的现象。

Method: 采用稀疏自编码器（SAEs）从ViT的CLS token表示中提取稀疏候选特征，然后比较不同epoch-层组合间的特征激活轮廓（activation profiles），从而跨两个维度（深度和时间）分析特征演化，并识别特征迁移现象。

Result: 特征迁移集中发生在训练早期，迁移方向更多朝向前层（较浅层）而非朝深层方向，随着特征组织逐渐稳定迁移减少。深层比浅层更早且更强烈地稳定下来。

Conclusion: 该框架可作为理解ViT学习和演化过程的工具，提供了超越表示级相似度度量的特征级洞察，揭示了训练过程中特征组织的动态规律。

Abstract: We present a novel view on feature evolution in Vision Transformers (ViTs) by visualizing the training process over two dimensions -- network depth (layer) and training time (epochs). We employ Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) to extract candidate sparse features from CLS-token representations and compare their activation profiles across epoch--layer pairs. This allows us to study feature-level dynamics that are not directly visible from representation-level similarity measures. Furthermore, we demonstrate how this framework of feature evolution allows us to describe feature migration, the change in the layer where a feature is most detectable during training. Our experiments show that migration is concentrated early in training, occurs more often toward earlier layers than toward deeper layers, and declines as feature organization stabilizes. We further find that deeper layers stabilize earlier and more strongly than shallow layers. The results show that our approach can be employed as a tool for understanding how ViTs learn and evolve.

</details>


### [52] [DPC-Net: Dual-Prior Collaborative Network for All-in-One Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20141)
*Zhaokun He,Kangbiao Shi,Axi Niu,Jian Jin,Peng Wu,Wei Dong,Qingsen Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Dual-Prior Collaborative Network（DPC-Net），一种统一图像修复网络，通过联合利用"退化-语义耦合先验"和"低级视觉先验"双先验，解决现有All-in-One Image Restoration方法中忽视语义信息、缺乏低级视觉先验导致的结构失真和语义不一致问题，在多个基准上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AiOIR方法存在两个关键问题：(1) 在退化建模中忽视了图像语义信息，导致恢复图像语义不一致；(2) 在重建阶段缺乏低级视觉先验引导，造成结构失真。这两个缺陷使得现有方法难以在统一模型中实现对多种退化的高质量修复。

Method: DPC-Net采用双分支协作架构：(1) Degradation-Aware Network (DAN)提取退化-语义耦合特征，通过Vision-Language Model (VLM)监督特征分布以引入语义信息；(2) Degradation-Semantic Modulation Module (DSMM)将VLM引导转化为退化-语义耦合表示并传播至解码器；(3) 解码阶段利用知识库提供低级视觉先验，由Dual-Prior Collaborative Reconstruction Module (DPCR)整合双先验信息以指导退化移除并保持结构和语义保真。

Result: 在多个图像修复基准数据集上的大量实验表明，DPC-Net相较于现有的SOTA AiOIR方法取得了更优的性能表现，验证了双先验协作策略的有效性。

Conclusion: DPC-Net通过将图像语义注入退化建模、并利用低级视觉先验引导重建过程，实现了双先验的协同互补，有效解决了统一图像修复中结构失真与语义不一致的问题。

Abstract: All-in-One Image Restoration (AiOIR) aims to handle diverse degradations within a unified model. However, existing methods often overlook image semantics in degradation modeling and lack low-level visual priors during reconstruction, leading to structural distortions and semantic inconsistencies. To address these issues, we propose a novel Dual-Prior Collaborative Network (DPC-Net), which achieves high-quality restoration by jointly exploiting degradation-semantic coupled priors and low-level visual priors. Specifically, degraded images are fed into a Degradation-Aware Network (DAN) to extract degradation-semantic coupled features. To this end, a Vision-Language Model (VLM) supervises DAN by constraining its features distribution, introducing image semantics into the encoding of degradation patterns. A Degradation-Semantic Modulation Module (DSMM) further translates this guidance into degradation-semantic coupling and propagates coupled representations to the decoder. During decoding, knowledge bases provide low-level visual priors, and the Dual-Prior Collaborative Reconstruction Module (DPCR) integrates dual-prior information to guide degradation removal while preserving structure and semantics, producing high-fidelity restored images. Extensive experiments on multiple restoration benchmarks demonstrate that DPC-Net achieves superior performance against state-of-the-art AiOIR methods.

</details>


### [53] [PelviNeXt: A Modality-Agnostic Hybrid Network for Pelvic Imaging in Women's Health](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20144)
*Siam Tahsin Bhuiyan,Rashedur Rahman,Sefatul Wasi,Halima Khatun,Ashraful Islam,AKM Mahbubur Rahman,Saadia Binte Alam,M Ashraful Amin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PelviNeXt，一种跨模态混合架构（结合密集卷积特征提取器、H-CBAM、MSFM和TH-MHSA），无需任务特定修改即可同时用于盆腔超声和X射线成像。在PCOSGen数据集上发现大量重复数据污染并发布去重版本，在PXR150骨折数据集上超越SOTA，证明单一架构可作为女性盆腔影像数据稀缺场景的可靠基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 女性健康在医学影像研究中的资源投入严重不足。多囊卵巢综合征（PCOS）和盆腔骨折等盆腔病理虽有重要临床意义，但缺乏公开、标注良好的基准数据集。作者希望构建一个无需任务特定修改、可跨模态应用的统一架构，同时解决数据稀缺和污染问题。

Method: 提出PelviNeXt混合架构，包含四个核心组件：密集卷积特征提取器、分层通道-空间注意力（H-CBAM）、多尺度融合模块（MSFM）和talking-heads多头自注意力（TH-MHSA）。该架构在不做修改的情况下同时应用于盆腔超声和X射线两种模态。此外，使用感知哈希（perceptual hashing）审计PCOSGen数据集中的重复/近似重复污染，并发布去重版本，建立5折交叉验证的完整性审计评估协议。

Result: 在PCOSGen数据集中发现大量精确及近似重复的污染数据并通过感知哈希完成审计，发布了去重版本并建立了首个完整性审计后的评估基线和协议。在PXR150骨盆骨折X射线数据集上，PelviNeXt在准确率、召回率、特异性和AUROC等指标上均超过此前报告的SOTA结果。消融实验确认每个架构组件在两个任务上均有贡献。

Conclusion: 单一架构无需任务特定修改即可作为盆腔影像跨模态的可靠基础，特别是在数据稀缺、研究不足的女性健康领域。这项工作同时贡献了去重数据集、完整性审计协议和跨模态强基模型。

Abstract: Women's health remains substantially under-resourced in medical imaging research, with pelvic pathologies such as polycystic ovary syndrome (PCOS) and pelvic fracture both suffering from a scarcity of public, well-annotated benchmark data despite their clinical importance. We introduce PelviNeXt, a modality-agnostic hybrid architecture combining a dense convolutional feature extractor, hierarchical channel-spatial attention (H-CBAM), a multi-scale fusion module (MSFM), and talking-heads multi-head self-attention (TH-MHSA), applied without modification to both pelvic ultrasound and X-ray inputs. While benchmarking PelviNeXt on PCOSGen, the only gynaecologist-annotated public PCOS ultrasound dataset, we identified extensive exact and near-duplicate contamination within and across the dataset. We audit this contamination via perceptual hashing, publicly release a deduplicated version of the dataset, and establish the first integrity-audited evaluation protocol and baseline for PCOSGen under 5-fold cross-validation. On the only publicly available pelvic fracture X-ray dataset (PXR150), PelviNeXt exceeds previously reported state-of-the-art results across accuracy, recall, specificity, and AUROC. Ablation studies confirm that each architectural component contributes to performance on both tasks. Our results demonstrate that a single architecture, applied without task-specific modification, can serve as a reliable foundation for pelvic imaging across modalities in data-scarce, under-researched areas of women's health.

</details>


### [54] [Artificial Intelligence for Workflow Analysis in Colorectal Surgery: A Multicentric, Cross-Procedural Development and Generalization Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20154)
*Pietro Mascagni,Julia Alekseenko,Pooja P Jain,Marta Goglia,Andrea Balla,Ludovica Baldari,Gianfranco Silecchia,Claudio Fiorillo,Vincenzo Tondolo,Salvador Morales-Conde,Luigi Boni,Sergio Alfieri,Nicolas Padoy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究提出AI-ColoWorkflow，一种用于微创结直肠手术（MIS-CRS）自动化手术工作流分析的深度学习模型。该模型结合微调的DINOv3视觉Transformer和分层多阶段时间卷积网络，在4个中心及公开数据集的多中心多术式数据上联合训练，实现手术阶段（Phase）和步骤（Step）的自动识别。模型在阶段识别上达到73.01%的macro F1，且在大部分实验中优于中心特异性和术式特异性模型，但在步骤识别上术式特异性模型仍有优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微创结直肠手术（MIS-CRS）存在显著的变异性且结局不一致。ColoWorkflow作为基于视频的手术工作流评估工具（VBA）虽已被验证，但人工VBA耗时费力，严重限制了其临床推广。因此迫切需要一种自动化深度学习模型来替代人工标注，实现高效、可扩展的手术工作流分析。

Method: 该研究收集了来自4个中心和公开数据集的MIS-CRS手术视频，依据ColoWorkflow框架进行阶段（Phase）和步骤（Step）的人工标注。构建的深度学习模型采用微调的DINOv3视觉Transformer对每帧进行视觉特征提取，结合分层多阶段时间卷积网络（hierarchical multi-stage temporal convolutional network），对阶段和步骤识别进行联合优化。模型在合并的多中心数据上训练（AI-ColoWorkflow），并与中心特异性和术式特异性模型在独立测试集上比较，评估指标包括macro F1、平衡准确率、精确率和召回率。

Result: AI-ColoWorkflow在阶段识别上达到macro F1为73.01%±10.27（平衡准确率73.43%），步骤识别macro F1为39.82%±7.06（平衡准确率38.65%）。全局模型在大多数实验中优于中心特异性和术式特异性模型，但术式特异性步骤识别除外。泛化分析中阶段识别的平均F1为48.42%。

Conclusion: AI-ColoWorkflow能够可靠识别MIS-CRS手术阶段。在合并的多中心、多术式数据上训练的单一模型在阶段识别上的泛化能力至少不弱于且通常优于中心或术式特异性模型；然而术式特异性步骤模型在特定术式上仍具优势，这表明未来手术AI开发应考虑混合训练策略。

Abstract: Minimally invasive colorectal surgeries (MIS-CRS) are characterised by significant variability and inconsistent outcomes. ColoWorkflow, a tool for the video-based assessment (VBA) of MIS-CRS workflow, was recently validated. However, manual VBA is time-consuming, limiting implementation. This study presents AI-ColoWorkflow, a deep learning model for automated surgical workflow analysis across MIS-CRS. Operative videos of MIS-CRS were collected from 4 centres and a publicly available dataset. Phases and steps were manually annotated according to ColoWorkflow. A deep learning model combining a fine-tuned DINOv3 vision transformer for per-frame visual feature extraction with a hierarchical multi-stage temporal convolutional network was jointly optimized for phase and step recognition. The model trained on pooled multicentric data, namely AI-ColoWorkflow was compared against centre-specific and procedure-specific models on a held-out test set. The following metrics were used for evaluation: macro F1 score, balanced accuracy, precision, and recall. AI-ColoWorkflow achieved a macro F1 of 73.01% $\pm$ 10.27 (balanced accuracy 73.43%) for phase recognition and 39.82% $\pm$ 7.06 (balanced accuracy 38.65%) for step recognition. The global model outperformed centre- and procedure-specific models in most experiments except procedure-specific step recognition. In the generalization analysis, mean F1 was 48.42% for phase recognition. AI-ColoWorkflow can reliably recognize MIS-CRS phases. A single model trained on pooled, multicentric, multi-procedural data generalises at least as well as and often better than centre- or procedure-specific models for phase recognition in MIS-CRS, while procedure-specific step models retain advantages for certain procedure types, motivating hybrid training strategies for future surgical AI development.

</details>


### [55] [G3Ego: Gaze-Guided Graphs for Egocentric Action Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20157)
*Marko Haralović,Akash Ramakrishnan,Estefania Talavera Martinez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出G3Ego，一种基于图的自我中心动作理解框架，利用视线（gaze）作为结构线索构建动作场景图，从稀疏采样帧中通过视觉-语言描述、物体基础和手部线索构建图，并利用佩戴者的视线剪枝不相关实体，实现高效的动作识别与预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自我中心动作理解依赖于在大量异中心数据上预训练的大型视频模型，计算开销大。然而许多第一人称动作仅依赖少数手-物交互和少量相关实体，现有方法未有效利用这一特性。

Method: 从稀疏采样帧中构建动作场景图：融合视觉-语言描述、基础物体和手部线索；使用摄像头佩戴者的视线直接作为图构建的一部分来剪枝无关实体；对图嵌入进行时间聚合用于动作识别与预测。与以往将视线作为辅助模态或注意力信号不同，G3Ego将视线直接融入图结构构建。

Result: 在EGTEA Gaze+和MECCANO数据集上，G3Ego与基于视频的方法相比达到竞争性性能，并在类别不平衡评估下持续提升Macro-F1分数，同时避免了昂贵的视频预训练依赖。

Conclusion: 视线引导的图表示在自我中心动作理解中有效且可解释，能够聚焦于动作相关交互，为无需大规模视频预训练的高效自我中心感知提供了新思路。

Abstract: Egocentric action understanding is often addressed using large video models pretrained on extensive exocentric datasets. However, many first-person actions depend on a small number of hand-object interactions involving only a few relevant entities.
  We propose G3Ego, a graph-based framework for egocentric action understanding that uses gaze as a structural cue to identify action-relevant entities in the scene. From sparsely sampled frames, G3Ego constructs action scene graphs from vision-language descriptions, grounded objects, and hand cues, and then prunes irrelevant entities using the camera wearer's gaze.
  The resulting graph embeddings are temporally aggregated for action recognition and anticipation. Unlike prior work that uses gaze primarily as an auxiliary modality or attention signal, G3Ego incorporates gaze directly into graph construction, producing efficient and interpretable representations focused on action-relevant interactions.
  Experiments on EGTEA Gaze+ and MECCANO show that G3Ego achieves competitive performance compared with video-based approaches and consistently improves Macro-F1 under class-imbalanced evaluation, while avoiding reliance on computationally expensive video pretraining. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of gaze-guided graph representations for egocentric action understanding.

</details>


### [56] [RoMAN-Flow: Taming Autoregressive Normalizing Flows for Offline Reinforcement Learning in Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20208)
*Shaoxuan Wang,Guangting Zheng,Rui Huang,Zhipeng Tang,Sha Zhang,Jiajun Deng,Yanyong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RoMAN-Flow是一个离线强化学习框架，通过无采样优势加权似然目标和一步式动作生成器蒸馏，解决了自回归归一化流策略在机器人操作中的采样瓶颈，兼顾策略性能与低推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线强化学习利用历史数据改进机器人策略而无需环境交互，但流行的扩散/流匹配策略缺乏可处理的似然，限制了基于似然的离线RL后训练。自回归归一化流（AR-NFs）兼具表达性动作建模和精确似然评估，但其顺序采样在策略优化和部署阶段产生巨大采样开销。

Method: RoMAN-Flow在策略优化阶段采用无采样的优势加权似然目标，直接对离线数据中高优势动作赋予更高似然而无需从自回归策略中采样；在部署阶段将优化后的自回归策略蒸馏为一步式动作生成器，实现低延迟动作预测。

Result: 在多个仿真操作基准和真实机器人平台上，RoMAN-Flow取得了有竞争力的策略性能，同时大幅降低了推理延迟。

Conclusion: RoMAN-Flow通过在策略优化和部署两个阶段分别解决采样瓶颈，使AR-NF策略在机器人操作中变得实用，实现了性能与推理效率的兼顾。

Abstract: Offline reinforcement learning improves robotic policies using previously collected data without further environment interaction. Yet prevalent diffusion- and flow-matching robot policies lack tractable likelihoods, limiting their use in likelihood-based offline RL post-training. AR-NFs offer both expressive action modeling and exact likelihood evaluation, but their sequential sampling incurs substantial sampling overhead during policy optimization and deployment. We present RoMAN-Flow (Robotic Manipulation with Autoregressive Normalizing Flows), an offline reinforcement learning framework that makes AR-NF policies practical for robotic manipulation by addressing this sampling bottleneck in both stages. During policy optimization, RoMAN-Flow employs a sampling-free, advantage-weighted likelihood objective that assigns higher likelihood to high-advantage actions from the offline dataset without sampling from the autoregressive policy. For efficient deployment, it distills the optimized autoregressive policy into a one-step action generator, enabling low-latency action prediction. Experiments across multiple simulated manipulation benchmarks and real-world robotic platforms demonstrate that RoMAN-Flow achieves competitive policy performance while substantially reducing inference latency. Code is available at https://github.com/konnyaku28/RoMAN-Flow.

</details>


### [57] [Unwarping the Lens: A Physics-Grounded Approach to Video Glasses Removal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20212)
*Radim Spetlik,David Futschik,Radek Danecek,Feitong Tan,Ziqian Bai,Rohit Pandey,Yinda Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出JFSnet框架，通过从商业生成模型（Nano Banana, Gemini 3 Pro Image）提取合成人脸图像，经三阶段结构过滤和物理光学模拟训练，实现高保真眼镜去除，在12,163张FFHQ子集上达到27.68 FPS，并在视频序列中优于扩散和GAN基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 眼镜去除面临面部几何被折射畸变和镜面反射遮挡的挑战；现有大尺度生成先验虽能进行静态图像修复，但缺乏维持身份、表情和姿态的结构约束，导致静态和动态序列中产生明显的"身份漂移"问题。

Method: 提出一种迁移框架：(1)从商业生成模型提取高保真合成人脸图像；(2)通过三阶段结构过滤流程正则化以保持身份、表情和姿态；(3)训练期间采用基于物理的镜头光学模拟以提供多样化的配对数据；(4)构建JFSnet（联合特征-空间网络），整合DINOv2语义特征与卷积解码器用于空间重建，利用平移等变约束提升时间一致性和高频细节保持。

Result: 在12,163张FFHQ子集上实现高保真度和结构准确性，推理速度27.68 FPS；在CelebV-Text视频序列的感知研究中，在眼部一致性、时间稳定性和整体复原质量上优于扩散和GAN基线。

Conclusion: 通过知识迁移和物理仿真约束，可以克服生成先验的随机性，实现兼顾高保真、结构准确与实时推理的眼镜去除方案，有效解决身份漂移问题。

Abstract: High-fidelity removal of eyeglasses from video is a major challenge in facial attribute editing, as the underlying facial geometry is often obscured by complex refractive distortions and view-dependent specular reflections. While large-scale generative priors have shown promise in eye-glasses removal via static image inpainting, they often lack the structural constraints necessary to maintain identity, expression, and pose, leading to visible "identity drift" in both static images and dynamic sequences. In this paper, we propose a novel transfer framework that addresses the stochastic nature of generative priors. Our pipeline first extracts high-fidelity synthetic face images from a commercial-grade generative model (Nano Banana, Gemini 3 Pro Image), regularizes them via a three-stage structural filtering process to preserve identity, expression, and pose, and finally applies physically-based simulation of lens optics during training to provide diverse, paired data. This process transfers Nano Banana's photo-realistic, multi-view knowledge into a specialized restoration architecture, JFSnet (Joint Feature-Spatial network). JFSnet integrates DINOv2-based semantic features with a convolutional decoder for spatial reconstruction, leveraging translation equivariance constraints to improve temporal consistency and high-frequency detail preservation. Evaluations on the curated Flickr-Faces-HQ (FFHQ) subset (12,163 images) show that our approach achieves high fidelity and structural accuracy, while maintaining inference speed of 27.68 FPS. In perceptual studies on CelebV-Text video sequences, our results are consistently preferred over diffusion and GAN-based baselines for ocular consistency, temporal stability, and overall restoration quality.

</details>


### [58] [Prompt-Conditioned Channel Attention for Hierarchical Feature Modulation toward Anatomy-Agnostic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20229)
*Mosharof Hossain,Md Rabiul Islam,Limon Halder,Erchin Serpedin,Md Kamrul Hasan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Prompt-Conditioned Channel Attention (PCCA)机制及PROMISE-Net框架，通过将语义提示深度融入编码器-解码器网络的分层通道注意力调制中，解决医学图像分割中低对比度、边界模糊和模态特异性伪影等挑战。PROMISE-Net包含CNN和Transformer两种变体，在四个医学影像基准上均显著优于基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的交互式分割方法主要通过晚期融合方式整合提示信息，缺乏在分层特征表示上进行提示驱动的通道级调制机制，难以捕捉深层上下文和模态特异性变化，从而限制了分割性能。

Method: 提出PCCA机制：通过池化提取紧凑的通道描述符，投影到共享空间，并通过门控激励机制融合以计算提示感知的通道注意力权重，实现对多网络阶段的特征响应进行自适应重校准。基于此构建PROMISE-Net，包含PROMISE-CNN（卷积）和PROMISE-Txformer（Transformer）两种网络变体。

Result: 在ISIC-Lesion、Kvasir-Polyp、CAMUS-Cardiac和Kvasir-Instrument四个基准上，PROMISE-CNN相比U-Net基线获得10.4%、8.7%、0.8%和3.4%的相对IoU提升；PROMISE-Txformer相比UNETR基线分别获得7.6%、23.0%、2.1%和1.1%的提升。

Conclusion: PCCA和PROMISE-Net在不同架构、成像模态和解剖目标上均取得一致改进，确立了其作为医学图像分割中提示感知分层特征调制的可扩展、可泛化框架的地位。

Abstract: Anatomically plausible segmentation remains challenging because of low contrast, ambiguous boundaries, and modality-specific artifacts. Interactive segmentation has emerged as a promising strategy to guide feature extraction and improve localization, particularly in structurally ambiguous regions. However, existing methods integrate prompts through late-stage fusion and lack explicit mechanisms for prompt-driven channel-wise modulation across hierarchical feature representations, limiting their ability to capture deeper contextual and modality-specific variations. To address these limitations, we introduce Prompt-Conditioned Channel Attention (PCCA), a novel modulation mechanism that enables deep, hierarchical integration of semantic prompts within encoder-decoder networks. PCCA extracts compact channel descriptors via pooling, projects them into a shared space, and fuses them through a gated excitation mechanism to compute prompt-aware channel attention weights. These weights adaptively recalibrate feature responses across multiple network stages, enabling prompt-conditioned, semantically enriched hierarchical representations. Building on this, we propose PROMISE-Net, instantiated in two network variants: a convolutional model (PROMISE-CNN) and a transformer-based model (PROMISE-Txformer). Across the ISIC-Lesion, Kvasir-Polyp, CAMUS-Cardiac, and Kvasir-Instrument benchmarks, integrating PCCA into PROMISE-CNN yielded relative IoU gains of 10.4%, 8.7%, 0.8%, and 3.4%, respectively, over the baseline U-Net, while PROMISE-Txformer achieved corresponding gains of 7.6%, 23.0%, 2.1%, and 1.1%, respectively, over the baseline UNETR. These results show consistent improvements across architectures, imaging modalities, and anatomical targets, establishing PCCA and PROMISE-Net as a scalable, generalizable framework for prompt-aware hierarchical feature modulation in medical image segmentation.

</details>


### [59] [Ultra-High-Definition Restoration Transformers with Correlation Matching Transformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20263)
*Cong Wang,Liyan Wang,Jinshan Pan,Wei Wang,Wenqi Ren,Jun Liu,Xiaochun Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UHDformer++是一个基于Transformer的通用框架，用于解决多种超高清（UHD）图像复原任务。它通过四个协调的学习空间（高分辨率HR、低分辨率LR、超分辨率SR和低高融合重建LHFR）工作，并引入FR-CMT和ACM两个模块桥接高低分辨率空间，在至少减少86%参数的同时，在五个UHD复原任务（低光增强、去雾、去模糊、去雨、去雪）上取得显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超高清（UHD）图像复原任务（如低光增强、去雾、去模糊、去雨、去雪）在计算上极具挑战性，现有方法通常需要大量模型参数且仅针对单一任务设计。作者希望构建一个通用、高效的Transformer框架，能够同时处理多种UHD复原任务，同时大幅降低模型参数量。

Method: UHDformer++包含四个协调的学习空间：1) HR空间用于多级特征提取并与低分辨率线索融合生成残差图像；2) LR空间用于学习紧凑且具代表性的特征；3) SR空间对LR输出进行上采样以提供更丰富的特征；4) LHFR空间用于最终图像复原。此外提出两个桥接模块：FR-CMT模块从高分辨率特征（max和mean池化融合）中选择top C/r个信息量最高的通道替换低分辨率Transformer中信息量较少的通道；ACM模块自适应地重新校准多尺度高分辨率特征，确保只有与任务相关的信息传播到LR空间。

Result: 与近期最先进方法相比，UHDformer++至少减少了86%的模型参数，同时在五个UHD复原任务（低光图像增强、去雾、去模糊、去雨、去雪）上取得了显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: UHDformer++证明了通过精心设计的四空间协调架构和通道级特征交互模块，可以实现一个既轻量又高性能的通用UHD图像复原框架，为UHD图像复原任务提供了一个高效统一的解决方案。

Abstract: We propose UHDformer++, a general Transformer-based framework to solve numerous Ultra-High-Definition (UHD) image restoration tasks. UHDformer++ operates across $4$ coordinated learning spaces: 1) a high-resolution space (HR) for multi-level feature extraction, 2) a low-resolution space (LR) for learning compact, representative features, 3) a super-resolution space (SR) for upsampling low-resolution features from SR, and 4) a low-high fusion and reconstruction space (LHFR) for final image restoration. Specifically, HR extracts multi-scale high-resolution features and fuses them with low-resolution cues to produce residual images, while LR distills complementary representations from HR to improve restoration quality. To supply LHFR with richer features, SR super-resolves LR outputs before fusion. We further introduce two modules to bridge the high- and low-resolution spaces. The Feature-Refined Correlation Matching Transformation (FR-CMT) module selects the top $C/r~(C~\text{denotes the number of channels;~}r\geq1~\text{controls the squeezing level})$, from the fusion between max- and mean-pooled high-resolution features to replace less informative channels in the low-resolution Transformer. The Adaptive Channel Modulator (ACM) adaptively recalibrates multi-scale high-resolution features, ensuring that only task-relevant information propagates to LR. Extensive experiments demonstrate that UHDformer++ reduces model parameters by at least 86\% compared with recent state-of-the-art methods while achieving substantial performance gains across $5$ UHD restoration tasks, including low-light image enhancement, dehazing, deblurring, deraining, and desnowing. Code will be released at https://github.com/supersupercong/uhdformerplus.

</details>


### [60] [Towards Surgical World-Action Modeling: A Preliminary Joint Visual-Trajectory Forecasting for Surgical Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20284)
*Weiliang Huang,Huanrong Liu,Bob Zhang,Qi Dou,Zhen Chen,Yun Gu,Guy Rosman,Qingbiao Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种初步的联合视觉-轨迹世界动作模型，从历史手术观察中同时预测未来视觉状态和器械轨迹，通过分块自回归策略显著提升了预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手术规划方法将未来场景生成和器械轨迹预测视为两个独立任务：仅场景模型无法在轨迹层面评估器械运动精度，仅轨迹模型无法捕捉器械运动的视觉后果，导致预测轨迹与未来场景演化之间的一致性被忽略。

Method: 将历史视频帧和器械轨迹编码为潜在表示，通过时空编码器处理后，分别由视觉状态预测头和轨迹预测头解码。基于该初步架构，采用分块自回归滚动预测策略迭代预测未来15步。

Result: 分块自回归策略在所有评估时域上均优于直接单次预测：首段PSNR从18.86提升至23.11 dB，ADE从45.77像素降至22.22像素，验证了联合视觉-运动预测的初步可行性。

Conclusion: 联合视觉-轨迹预测展现初步可行性，但长预测时域上存在视觉逐步退化与轨迹误差累积问题，是未来手术世界动作建模需克服的关键挑战。

Abstract: Reliable surgical planning requires models to anticipate not only how instruments will move, but also how the operative visual state will evolve together with such motion. Existing approaches typically treat future scene generation and instrument trajectory prediction as two separate tasks. Scene-only models cannot directly evaluate the accuracy of future instrument motion at the trajectory level, while trajectory-only models fail to capture the visual consequences of instrument movement, leaving the consistency between predicted trajectories and future scene evolution unaddressed. Jointly forecasting both provides a more complete account of surgical action-scene dynamics by enabling explicit trajectory-level evaluation while simultaneously modeling the corresponding visual evolution. To bridge this gap, we present a preliminary joint visual-trajectory world-action model that simultaneously forecasts future visual states and instrument trajectories from historical surgical observations. Specifically, we encode historical video frames and tool trajectories into latent representations, which are processed by a temporal-spatial encoder and subsequently decoded through separate visual-state and trajectory prediction heads. Based on this preliminary architecture, a chunked autoregressive rollout is repeatedly applied to predict fifteen future steps. The chunked strategy consistently outperforms direct one-shot prediction across all evaluated horizons, improving first-segment PSNR from 18.86 to 23.11 dB and reducing ADE from 45.77 to 22.22 pixels. These results demonstrate the initial feasibility of joint visual-motion forecasting. However, we observe progressive visual degradation and accumulated trajectory errors over longer prediction horizons, which remain important challenges for future surgical world-action modeling.

</details>


### [61] [CalcSeg: Confidence-aware 3D Latent Context Curriculum Learning For Myocardial Scar Segmentation From Single-Stack LGE-CMRs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20305)
*Nivetha Jayakumar,Hannah Kim,Amit R. Patel,Miaomiao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CalcSeg，一个基于置信度感知的潜在上下文课程学习框架，用于从单堆栈2D LGE-CMR图像中进行心肌疤痕分割。通过动态半监督课程学习策略和潜在切片级自注意力机制，该方法在多中心临床数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单堆栈LGE-CMR心肌疤痕分割长期面临挑战，主要因为低组织对比度、弥散性和小疤痕区域的存在，以及3D空间上下文信息有限。这些因素严重影响了分割的准确性和可靠性。

Method: 1) 提出置信度感知的动态半监督课程学习策略，通过集成预测疤痕图的误差、量化认知不确定性和疤痕负担估计，自动评估样本难度，无需人工标签即可渐进式地从简单到困难样本进行训练；2) 开发潜在切片级自注意力机制，从稀疏2D输入中捕获切片间依赖关系并推断3D空间表示。

Result: 在多中心临床LGE-CMR数据集上，CalcSeg在所有对比方法中一致取得最优性能，尤其在临床具有挑战性的病例上表现出显著的提升。

Conclusion: CalcSeg通过置信度感知的课程学习与潜在3D上下文融合，有效解决了单堆栈LGE-CMR图像中疤痕分割的难点，代码已开源。

Abstract: Myocardial scar segmentation from single-stack late gadolinium-enhanced cardiac magnetic resonance (LGE-CMR) imaging has been a longstanding and clinically important challenge, particularly in the presence of low tissue contrast, diffuse, and small scar regions. These challenges are further intensified by the limited availability of 3D spatial context. This paper presents CalcSeg, a Confidence-aware latent context curriculum learning framework that leverages fused 3D feature representations from single-stack 2D LGE-CMR images for robust scar segmentation. Specifically, we introduce a dynamic semi-supervised curriculum learning strategy that progressively expands training from easier to more challenging scar cases using a learned confidence-aware scoring function. Such a function integrates errors in the predicted scar maps with quantified epistemic uncertainty and scar burden estimation to automatically assess sample difficulty without requiring manual labels. To compensate for the limited spatial context in single-stack acquisitions, we then develop a latent slice-wise self-attention to capture inter-slice dependencies and infer 3D spatial representations from sparse 2D inputs. We evaluate CalcSeg on multi-center clinical LGE-CMR datasets and benchmark against existing scar segmentation networks. Experimental results show that CalcSeg consistently outperforms all competing methods, particularly with substantial improvements on clinically challenging cases. Our code is released on Github.

</details>


### [62] [DreamHand: Repurposing Video Diffusion Models for Occlusion-Robust Egocentric 3D Hand Motion Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20308)
*Yufei Liu,Xixi Wang,Hao Li,Ganlong Zhao,Kaitong Cai,Chengkai Jin,Chunxiao Liu,Jianbo Liu,Siyuan Huang,Xingang Pan,Hongsheng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DreamHand是一个离线片段级框架，将视频扩散模型重构为确定性几何编码器，通过单次前向传播从干净潜在空间中提取特征，恢复被遮挡和出视野的手部3D轨迹，在五个基准上达到SOTA，MPJPE-p降低30%-61%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一人称视频为具身AI提供可扩展的操作数据，但恢复度量级3D手部轨迹面临严重物体遮挡和频繁出视野间隙的挑战。现有的单帧和窗口化时间回归器在手部短暂离开画面时失败，而最近的视频扩散模型（VDMs）作为像素空间渲染器依赖沉重且随机的多步采样。

Method: DreamHand将VDM重定向为确定性几何编码器，通过一种确定性干净潜在编码器（Deterministic Clean-Latent Encoder）对干净潜在表示进行单次前向传播，以暴露超出当前观测的场景内容（包括遮挡和出视野的手部）。随后使用双向时空解码器（Bidirectional Spatiotemporal Decoder）解码特征，恢复连续的双手轨迹，具有度量尺度放置且无需外部检测器。此外，基于射线的相机求解器（Ray-Based Camera Solver）支持无需测试时相机内参的第二配置。

Result: 在五个自我中心基准上，DreamHand达到新的最先进水平，在遮挡严重的ARCTIC上将MPJPE-p降低30%，在HOT3D上降低40%。当评估中包含出视野手部时，增益达到46%-61%。

Conclusion: DreamHand提供了一条从日常人类视频扩展到机器人操作数据的可扩展路径，通过高效的确定性推断解决了遮挡和出视野手部轨迹恢复的难题。

Abstract: Egocentric video offers scalable manipulation data for embodied AI, yet recovering metric 3D hand trajectories remains challenging due to severe object occlusion and frequent out-of-sight gaps. Existing single-frame and windowed temporal regressors fail when hand shortly leaves the frame, while recent video diffusion models (VDMs) rely on heavy, stochastic multi-step sampling as pixel-space renderers. We instead repurpose VDM into a deterministic geometry encoder. A single forward pass over the clean latent exposes scene content beyond current observations, including occluded and out-of-sight hands. We introduce DreamHand, an offline clip-level framework that extracts features via a Deterministic Clean-Latent Encoder and decodes them with a Bidirectional Spatiotemporal Decoder. DreamHand recovers continuous bimanual trajectories with metric placement and no external detector, while a Ray-Based Camera Solver supports a second configuration that needs no test-time camera intrinsics. Across five egocentric benchmarks, DreamHand sets a new state of the art, cutting MPJPE-p by 30% on occlusion-heavy ARCTIC and 40% on HOT3D. These gains reach 46%-61% once out-of-sight hands are included in the evaluation, offering a scalable path from everyday human video to robot manipulation data.

</details>


### [63] [Inter-X++: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Multimodal Human-Human Interaction Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20312)
*Liang Xu,Chengqun Yang,Zili Lin,Xintao Lv,Yichao Yan,Xin Jin,Zhibo Chen,Xiaokang Yang,Wenjun Zeng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Inter-X++，一个大规模高保真人-人交互（HHI）基准数据集，包含11,388个交互序列和超过810万帧，配备了包括细粒度文本描述、类别、因果顺序、关系性格、接触图等多维标注，并构建了统一的OpenHHI表示与建模框架，在生成与感知任务上均达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HHI数据集受限于低保真运动学信息、缺乏灵巧手部手势和丰富的多模态标注；同时交互表示碎片化和评估协议不一致，阻碍了公平严谨的基准测试。

Method: 通过新型混合动作捕捉系统采集高保真交互序列；提供层级细粒度文本描述、交互类别、因果交互顺序、主体关系与个性、顶点级接触图和物理约束等多维标注；构建涵盖生成与感知两大范式的四类下游任务统一测试基准；提出OpenHHI统一表示与建模框架，联合优化交互重建和语义理解。

Result: OpenHHI在生成和感知任务上均取得最先进性能，证明统一表示能够同时桥接交互理解与生成。

Conclusion: Inter-X++作为全面的大规模HHI基准，系统解决了当前领域的瓶颈问题，Unified表示框架OpenHHI验证了统一建模在交互生成与感知方面的有效性。

Abstract: The capability to perceive and synthesize human-human interactions is fundamental to developing intelligent digital human systems. However, existing datasets and modeling approaches are fundamentally constrained by low-fidelity kinematics, the omission of dexterous hand gestures and a severe lack of rich multimodal annotations. Furthermore, fragmented interaction representations and inconsistent evaluation protocols also impede fair and rigorous benchmarking. To systematically address these bottlenecks, we present Inter-X++, a comprehensive and large-scale benchmark designed to empower versatile HHI analysis. Captured via a novel hybrid motion capture system, Inter-X++ provides 11,388 high-fidelity interaction sequences and over 8.1M frames, featuring precise whole-body movements and detailed finger articulations. Meanwhile, we enrich the data foundation with multifaceted annotations, including hierarchical fine-grained textual descriptions, interaction categories, causal interaction orders, the relationship and personality of the subjects, as well as vertex-level contact maps and physically regularized constraints. Leveraging these elaborate annotations, we formulate a unified testing ground comprising four categories of downstream tasks that symmetrically span both generative and perceptive paradigms. To eliminate benchmarking ambiguities, we systematically standardize the interaction representations and evaluation protocols. Finally, we go beyond dataset construction to propose OpenHHI, a single and unified HHI representation and modeling framework that jointly optimizes interaction reconstruction and semantic understanding. Extensive experiments reveal that OpenHHI achieves state-of-the-art performance on both generation and perception tasks. This definitively proves that our unified representation successfully bridges interaction understanding and generation simultaneously.

</details>


### [64] [Swift-Image: Exploring the Performance Frontier of Compact Unified Image Generation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20334)
*Taihang Hu,Zhao Wang,Zuan Gao,Tao Liu,Hao Yan,Zhengze Xu,Yuhang Yu,Yongchao Du,Xingjian Wang,Jun Zheng,Qinye Zhou,Zhengrui Chen,Chao Lin,Yefeng Shen,Zhengtao Wu,Ge Wu,Xiaoli Xu,Denghui Yang,Huayu Zhang,Mingzhou Zhang,Mengting Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Swift-Image，一个紧凑统一的6B参数单流DiT模型，支持文生图、单图编辑和多图编辑，通过渐进式训练流程、并行专家RL与多教师在线蒸馏、Prompt Enhancer以及结构剪枝和少步蒸馏，在仅243K GPU训练小时下达到开源模型领先的聚合性能，压缩的3B模型几乎无性能损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在有限的计算预算下，探索一个相对较小的视觉生成模型能通过系统性的训练工程被推进到何种程度，验证小型模型在统一生成-编辑任务上可以达到的潜力。

Method: 采用6B单流DiT架构；设计渐进式训练管道（从广泛语义覆盖到高分辨率、强视觉质量和统一生成-编辑监督）；后训练阶段使用并行专家强化学习与多教师在线策略蒸馏以缓解异构目标间的干扰；引入Prompt Enhancer将用户请求解耦为高层次推理与像素级渲染，翻译为生成器对齐的视觉规格；通过结构剪枝和少步蒸馏得到3B和加速变体。

Result: Swift-Image在开源模型中取得领先的聚合性能，仅用6B参数和243K GPU训练小时；压缩的3B模型性能几乎无损失，少步蒸馏在显著减少采样步数的同时进一步提升聚合编辑性能。

Conclusion: 系统性训练工程可以显著提升紧凑模型在统一生成与编辑任务上的表现，并总结了架构、数据课程、后训练、提示增强和模型压缩方面的实用经验。

Abstract: We present Swift-Image, a compact unified model for text-to-image generation, single-image editing, and multi-image editing. Our goal is to explore how far a relatively small visual generator can be pushed through systematic training engineering under a constrained computational budget. Swift-Image adopts an efficient 6B single-stream DiT and a progressive training pipeline that evolves from broad semantic coverage to higher resolution, stronger visual quality, and unified generation-editing supervision. For post-training, we employ parallel expert reinforcement learning followed by multi-teacher on-policy distillation to alleviate interference among heterogeneous objectives. We further decouple high-level reasoning from pixel-level rendering with a Prompt Enhancer that translates user requests into generator-aligned visual specifications. For efficient deployment, structural pruning and few-step distillation produce 3B and accelerated variants. Swift-Image achieves leading aggregate performance among evaluated open-source models with only 6B parameters and 243K GPU training hours; the compressed 3B model incurs nearly no loss, while few-step distillation further improves aggregate editing performance with substantially fewer sampling steps. Our study also summarizes practical lessons for architecture, data curriculum, post-training, prompt enhancement, and model compression.

</details>


### [65] [4DAnyone: Create Anyone in 4D from a Casual Monocular Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20335)
*Yudong Jin,Tao Xie,Qihang Zhang,Zehong Shen,Zhen Xu,Yujun Shen,Hujun Bao,Xiaowei Zhou,Yinghao Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 4DAnyone是一个从非标定单目视频中重建4D人体的框架，通过生成重建级多视角一致视频并将其提升为4D高斯溅射（4DGS）。针对现有视频扩散模型在目标视角数量扩展时无法保持一致性的"有界注意力上下文"问题，提出参考上下文打包（RCP）和目标上下文路由（TCR）两种互补设计，分别以O(1)复杂度压缩参考视角上下文并轮流路由目标视角分组以共享跨组信息，最终在DNA-Rendering和DyMVHumans基准上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有相机控制的视频扩散模型在生成少量新颖视角视频时表现良好，但扩展到4DGS重建所需的数十个目标视角时无法保持多视角一致性。作者将这一失败归因于"有界注意力上下文"问题：当目标视角超出单个DiT前向传播的容量时，视角必须分组处理，从而暴露两个耦合瓶颈——参考上下文侧的条件信息随视角数量线性增长（O(N)）导致跨视角外观引导弱化，目标上下文侧的分组间无法直接交换信息导致全局结构漂移。

Method: 4DAnyone提出两种互补设计：(1) 参考上下文打包（RCP）将不断增长的参考视角压缩为固定长度的混合分辨率上下文，实现O(1)参考上下文复杂度；(2) 目标上下文路由（TCR）在去噪过程中轮转目标视角分组，在高噪声步骤共享上下文以保持全局一致性，在低噪声步骤稳定细节。此外构建了基于内部游戏引擎的MVGameHuman数据集，并与光舞台和野外视频数据集联合训练。

Result: 在DNA-Rendering和DyMVHumans基准上，4DAnyone在新颖视角视频质量和下游4DGS重建两方面均优于现有方法，并展现出鲁棒的野外泛化能力。

Conclusion: 4DAnyone通过将视频扩散模型的上下文瓶颈解耦为参考侧与目标侧两个问题并分别设计解决方案，实现了从单目视频到4DGS重建的完整框架，验证了扩散生成与显式三维表示结合的有效性，且具备实际工程可用的泛化性能。

Abstract: We present 4DAnyone, a framework for reconstructing 4D humans from an uncalibrated monocular video by generating reconstruction-grade multiview-consistent videos and lifting them into 4D Gaussian Splatting (4DGS). Existing camera-controlled video diffusion models synthesize plausible novel-view videos but fail to maintain consistency when scaled to the tens of target views required for 4DGS reconstruction. We identify this failure as a bounded-attention-context problem: when target views exceed the capacity of a single DiT forward pass, they must be split into groups, exposing two coupled bottlenecks. On the reference-context side, conditioning on all previously generated views grows as $O(N)$, weakening cross-view appearance guidance. On the target-context side, disjoint groups cannot directly exchange information, causing global structural drift. 4DAnyone addresses both bottlenecks with two complementary designs: Reference Context Packing (RCP) compresses growing reference views into a fixed-length mixed-resolution context with $O(1)$ reference-context complexity, while Target Context Routing (TCR) rotates target-view groupings during denoising to share context across groups at high-noise steps and stabilize details at low-noise steps. We further build the MVGameHuman dataset using our in-house game engine and combine it with light-stage and in-the-wild video datasets for training. Experiments on DNA-Rendering and DyMVHumans show that 4DAnyone outperforms prior methods in both novel-view video quality and downstream 4DGS reconstruction, with robust in-the-wild generalization. See our project page for video results and source code: https://4danyone.github.io.

</details>


### [66] [WithEveryone: Unified Planning and Identity Grounding for Group Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20336)
*Hengyuan Xu,Qixun Wang,Yiji Cheng,Miles Yang,Zhao Zhong,Wei Cheng,Xingjun Ma,Yu-gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对包含多个指定人物的群体图像生成任务，提出统一框架WithEveryone，通过地址化token注入身份、预测结构化的身份-布局计划并将其作为视觉条件，配合Layout-Grounded ID Loss和ID Representation Forcing两项核心目标函数，在至多10个参考身份的群体图像生成上取得先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当场景中必须包含多个指定人物时，身份保持的图像生成变得不可靠。除了保留每个身份外，模型还需将每个参考人物绑定到不同的具体人物和位置，而训练时的身份损失需要在多个包含噪声的预测人脸之间建立对应关系，这构成了现有方法难以克服的挑战。

Method: WithEveryone框架包含三个核心组件：1) 将每个选中的身份作为地址化token（addressed token）注入模型；2) 预测结构化的身份-布局计划（identity-layout plan）并将其渲染为视觉条件；3) 两个关键目标函数——Layout-Grounded ID Loss利用标注的人脸区域直接监督预期身份（避免不稳定的基于嵌入的人脸匹配），以及ID Representation Forcing在图像合成前为每个身份训练预测。

Result: 在身份不相交的基准测试中，WithEveryone取得了最高的目标上下文身份相似度，将人脸相似度从GPT-Image-2的0.462提升至0.499，将复制粘贴伪影从0.169降至0.055。同时覆盖了97.3%的请求身份，重复率仅为2.8%。

Conclusion: 显式的身份-布局grounding能够使身份保持的图像生成扩展到更大的群体规模，且无需依赖直接参考人脸复制，验证了该方法的有效性。

Abstract: Identity-preserving image generation becomes increasingly unreliable when a scene must contain many specified people. Beyond retaining each identity, the model must bind every reference to a distinct person and location, while training-time identity losses must establish correspondence among several noisy predicted faces. We introduce WithEveryone, a unified framework for generating group images up to ten reference identities. WithEveryone injects each selected identity as an addressed token, predicts a structured identity--layout plan, and renders the plan as a visual condition. Its key objective, Layout-Grounded ID Loss, uses annotated face regions to supervise the intended identities directly, avoiding unstable embedding-based face matching; ID Representation Forcing additionally trains a prediction for each identity before image synthesis. On an identity-disjoint benchmark, WithEveryone achieves the highest target-context identity similarity, improving face similarity from 0.462 for GPT-Image-2 to 0.499, while reducing copy-paste artifacts from 0.169 to 0.055. It further covers 97.3\% of the requested identities with a duplicate rate of only 2.8\%. These results show that explicit identity--layout grounding enables identity-preserving generation to scale to larger groups without relying on direct reference-face copying.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

### [67] [Active Inference as Context Acquisition for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19202)
*Sanchayan Dutta,Sai Niranjan Ramachandran,Suvrit Sra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于主动推理（active inference）的框架，用于解决AI智能体在上下文获取中的"默认假设 vs 澄清/检索/工具调用"的权衡问题。内部推理步骤更新对潜在任务状态的信念，外部决策选择下一个上下文动作、任务动作或停止动作以最小化期望自由能，并在确定性条件下简化为按token成本归一化的期望信息增益。作者将其实例化为OQA框架，在25到300个候选项的二元和多分类任务上对前沿语言模型进行了基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式AI智能体需要尽可能高效地获取正确的上下文。当用户省略约束、偏好、文件或任务变量时，智能体可以选择默认假设，也可以花费token进行澄清提问、检索调用、工具调用或提示尝试。这种权衡需要一个形式化的决策框架来指导智能体在"成本"和"信息收益"之间做出最优选择。

Method: 将上下文获取问题形式化为主动推断：内部推理步骤在潜在任务状态上更新信念（inner inference），外部决策选择下一个上下文动作、任务动作或停止动作以最小化期望自由能（outer decision）。在确定性设置下，认知项（epistemic term）简化为期望信息增益，并可选按token成本进行归一化。框架实例化为Optimal Question Asking (OQA)，使用精确后验和动态规划预言机（oracle）。

Result: 在25到300个候选项的二元和多分类任务上对前沿语言模型进行了基准测试，并研究了token预算下的"澄清后生成"（clarification before generation）以及自动化提示优化（automated prompt optimization）。

Conclusion: 该框架是模型无关的（model-agnostic），将主动推理视为AI智能体上下文获取层的设计原则，而不仅仅是算法实现细节。

Abstract: Interactive AI agents must acquire the right context as efficiently as possible. When a user omits a constraint, preference, file, or task variable, an agent can proceed with a default assumption or spend tokens on a clarifying question, retrieval call, tool call, or prompt trial. We formulate this tradeoff as active inference for context acquisition. An inner inference step updates beliefs over a latent task state, and an outer decision selects the next context action, task action, or stop action to minimize expected free energy under cost. In deterministic settings, the epistemic term reduces to expected information gain, optionally normalized by token cost. We instantiate the framework in Optimal Question Asking (OQA), with exact posteriors and a dynamic programming oracle, and benchmark frontier language models on binary and multiway categorical tasks from 25 to 300 candidates. We also study clarification before generation and automated prompt optimization under token budgets. The formulation is model-agnostic and views active inference as a design principle for the context-acquisition layer of AI agents.

</details>


### [68] [Robust Metaheuristics under Uncertainty for Berth Allocation and Quay Crane Assignment: A Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19214)
*Yang Li,Peilan Xu,Wenjian Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是首个专门针对不确定性条件下泊位分配与岸桥调度问题（BACAP）的鲁棒群体元启发式优化方法的综述，系统梳理了不确定性来源、信息表示、机制导向的方法归类、基准测试套件及开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在港口运营中，BACAP的不确定性（如船舶到达偏差、装卸时间波动、资源中断）会使名义假设下的调度方案在执行中变得脆弱，而现有关于鲁棒元启发式优化BACAP的研究在不确定性表示、鲁棒性准则、搜索机制和实验评估协议上较为分散，缺乏系统性综述。

Method: 从机制导向的视角组织现有方法，涵盖四个维度：解表示与解码、鲁棒评估与选择、鲁棒性引导的搜索动力学、可行性保持与恢复。同时提出一个面向不确定BACAP的基准测试套件，并报告了代表性元启发式算法结合不同鲁棒策略的基线实验结果。

Result: 首次提供了针对鲁棒群体元启发式BACAP的专门综述，构建了统一的机制分类框架，提出了支持受控实证比较的基准套件，并给出了基线实验结果。

Conclusion: 识别了四个开放挑战：基准扩展、鲁棒感知的搜索设计、时间自适应鲁棒性以及非平稳不确定性，为未来研究提供了方向。

Abstract: The berth allocation and quay crane assignment problem (BACAP) is a representative port-terminal scheduling problem in maritime transportation and freight logistics, where vessel arrivals, berth positions, service durations, and quay?crane availability are tightly coupled. Under uncertainties such as arrival deviations, handling-time fluctuations, and resource disruptions, schedules optimized under nominal assumptions may become fragile during execution, motivating the study of robust metaheuristic optimization for BACAP in port-terminal operations. Although population-based metaheuristics have been widely used for BACAP and related port-scheduling problems, existing studies remain fragmented in their uncertainty repre?sentations, robustness criteria, search mechanisms, and empir?ical evaluation protocols. To the best of our knowledge, this paper provides the first focused review dedicated to robust population-based metaheuristics for BACAP under uncertainty. We first summarize uncertainty sources and information repre?sentations in BACAP, and then organize existing methods from a mechanism-oriented perspective, covering solution representation and decoding, robust evaluation and selection, robustness-guided search dynamics, and feasibility preservation and recovery. We further present a benchmark suite for uncertain BACAP to support controlled empirical comparison and report illustrative baseline results by combining representative metaheuristics with different robustness strategies. Finally, we identify open chal?lenges related to benchmark extension, robustness-aware search design, time-adaptive robustness, and non-stationary uncertainty.

</details>


### [69] [How to Navigate Uncertainty About AI Consciousness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19215)
*Dr Tom McClelland*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将AI意识研究中难以解决的"是否具有意识"问题，转向更可处理的"是否具有情感效价（valence）"问题，以此为基础为潜在有感知能力的AI建立负责任的开发方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在AI是否可能具有意识存在深度不确定性的背景下，我们面临两难：假设AI无感知可能对其造成严重伤害，假设AI有感知则可能浪费资源在无感知的机器上。由于AI意识问题本质上是棘手的（intractable），这一困境难以摆脱。

Method: 作者提出将研究焦点从不可处理的AI意识问题转移到可处理的AI效价问题——即评估AI是否具有那些若其有意识时构成效价体验的状态。通过这种转变，可以建立充分的责任基础来指导潜在有感知AI的开发。

Result: 作者论证了对AI效价状态的评估足以支撑一种负责任的处理潜在有感知AI的方法，从而为上述两难困境提供了一条出路。

Conclusion: 与其纠缠于AI意识的形而上学难题，不如聚焦于可操作、可评估的效价问题，这为处理潜在有感知AI提供了切实可行的伦理和负责任路径。

Abstract: Given deep uncertainty about the possibility of artificial consciousness, it is unclear how we should treat potentially sentient AI. On the one hand, we could assume insentience but risk doing terrible harms to entities that deserve moral standing. On the other hand, we could assume sentience and instead risk wasting resources on insentient machines. The intractability of questions around AI consciousness mean that this dilemma is hard to escape. I suggest a way out of that shifts from intractable questions of AI consciousness to tractable questions of AI valence. Specifically, we can assess whether an AI has states that would constitute valenced experiences if it were conscious. I show how this is sufficient to ground a responsible approach to the development of potentially conscious AI.

</details>


### [70] [Bounded Sovereignty and the Control Tax: Pricing AI Oversight When the Deployer Does Not Own the Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19216)
*Zhen Wen Lim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"有界主权"概念，分析在API/托管端点上部署前沿模型时，部署者对数据、模型、基础设施和交互四层的部分访问权限如何决定AI控制协议的可执行性，并通过135万次合成病例模拟验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI控制研究多假设部署者可深度改造模型及其流水线，但受监管组织通过API使用前沿模型时往往无法控制模型权重、服务基础设施、内部追踪和完整交互日志。这些访问条件的缺失使许多控制协议在实践中无法执行，因此需要系统化地理解访问权限对控制协议可行性的约束。

Method: 论文提出四层访问类型学（数据/模型/基础设施/交互层），构建协议-层级需求矩阵，引入"主权折扣成本"概念（为弥补缺失访问而通过合同、架构、审计、供应商保证、残余风险或缩减系统范围所付出的控制成本），并开展135万次合成病例的访问消融实验，辅以匿名国家支付基础设施场景进行解释性分析（作为构念效度验证，非真实支付系统证据）。

Result: 结果显示：完整日志可改善诊断能力，执行前网关可支持干预，追踪访问与模型版本控制可增强事后解释，而缩减系统范围可在提升安全性的同时降低可用性。

Conclusion: 被作为通用安全方案提出的控制协议应明确声明其访问假设；不同访问条件决定了哪些控制协议在特定部署场景中实际可行。

Abstract: AI control research asks how to deploy models safely even when they may be misaligned, but many control protocols assume that the deployer can instrument the model and its surrounding pipeline. That assumption often fails for regulated organisations using frontier models through APIs or managed endpoints, where the deployer may control the business process but not the model weights, serving infrastructure, internal traces, update process, or full interaction logs. This paper introduces bounded sovereignty: partial technical and contractual access across the data, model, infrastructure, and interaction layers of the AI stack. It argues that these access conditions determine which control protocols can be executed in practice. The paper contributes a four-layer access typology, a protocol-by-layer requirements matrix, and the concept of sovereignty discount cost: the part of the control tax spent substituting for missing access through contracts, architecture, audit, vendor assurance, residual risk, or reduced system scope. It also reports a synthetic access-ablation experiment over 1.35 million synthetic case simulations and interprets the findings through an anonymised national-payments-infrastructure scenario. The experiment is not real-world payment-system evidence; it is a construct-validity exercise. The results show that complete logs improve diagnosis, a pre-execution gateway enables intervention, trace access and model-version control strengthen post-incident explanation, and scope restriction can improve safety while reducing usefulness. Control protocols proposed as general safety solutions should therefore state their access assumptions explicitly.

</details>


### [71] [Interaction valence reveals contrasting social networks in dairy cattle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19222)
*Sibi Parivendan,Suresh Raja Neethirajan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出一种基于价值的社交网络框架，利用姿态估计计算机视觉管线将奶牛的交互行为（亲和型与攻击型）转换为群体层面的网络表征，并在商业奶牛场的挤奶前区域视频数据上展示了该方法能够区分两类行为网络、揭示聚合计数掩盖的行为结构差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社会关系影响动物获取资源的机会、冲突暴露和群体稳定性，但现有的自动化牲畜监测通常将行为视为孤立事件，缺乏对行为价值（亲和vs攻击）的区分，导致无法全面理解畜群的社会结构。

Method: 采用姿态估计的计算机视觉管线分析7小时39分钟的连续视频；经过质量控制后保留36头奶牛、177个二元组的1,183个候选交互；将交互按预测价值分为亲和型与攻击型两类，构建两类分离的网络层，并进行社区检测（模块度Q=0.429）和网络属性分析（密度、传递性、平均路径长度、介数中心性等）。

Result: 审计样本中自动化与人工标注一致率为82.8%，未加权宏F1为0.872；网络连通（密度0.281、传递性0.513、平均路径长度1.88）；攻击型交互占保留事件的72.4%和交互时长的76.0%；具有最多伴侣的奶牛并非最高介数中心性；分离亲和型与攻击型网络层产生了不同的边集、社区划分和个体位置。

Conclusion: 聚合的交互计数可能掩盖网络的行为组成，价值感知分析为研究竞争、亲和与福利相关变化提供了框架，但在作为福利或健康指标使用前需要纵向验证。

Abstract: Social relationships shape access to resources, exposure to conflict and group stability, yet automated livestock monitoring typically treats behaviour as isolated events. Here, we present a valence-aware social-network framework that transforms video-derived interactions into herd-level representations of affiliative and agonistic organization. A pose-based computer-vision pipeline analysed 7 h 39 min of continuous video from the pre-milking area of one commercial dairy farm. After quality control, 1,183 of 1,414 candidate interactions remained, involving 36 cows and 177 dyads. In a predicted-class-balanced audit of 198 pipeline-detected clips, automated and manual labels agreed in 82.8% of cases, with an unweighted audit-sample macro-F1 of 0.872. These values describe the audited sample rather than prevalence-weighted or end-to-end deployment performance. The aggregated network was connected (density = 0.281; transitivity = 0.513; mean path length = 1.88), and predicted affiliative events formed five algorithmic communities (modularity Q = 0.429). Within the observed zone, predicted agonistic interactions comprised 72.4% of retained events and 76.0% of interaction duration. The cow with the most partners did not have the highest betweenness centrality. Separating events by predicted valence produced descriptively different affiliative and agonistic layers, with contrasting edge sets, community partitions and individual positions. Thus, pooled interaction counts can obscure the behavioural composition of an observed network. Valence-aware analysis provides a framework for testing hypotheses about competition, affiliation and welfare-relevant change, while requiring longitudinal validation before use as a welfare or health indicator.

</details>


### [72] [Air Traffic Control Using Large Language Models: Prompt Engineering, Architecture, and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19299)
*Mahyar Ghazanfari,Matthias Casanova,Jordan Kam,Alex Zongo,Peng Wei,Torsten Darrell,Alexandre Bayen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文实验评估了大型语言模型（LLM）能否生成操作上逼真的空中交通管制（ATC）通话。研究者以旧金山"海湾之旅"航线上的通用航空飞行人工转录作为地面真值，设计了五种约束递增的提示结构，嵌入有状态的多轮对话流水线中评估九个开源和闭源LLM。结果表明：提供已转录示例能提升相似度，但收紧提示反而无益；最轻量的提示表现最佳，而最严格脚本化的提示因自身错误在对话中累积而崩溃，注入正确历史可以修复。论文指出了LLM辅助ATC的可行路径及当前局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ATC通信是安全关键的对话场景，在航空交通管理其他环节已半自动化的情况下仍然主要依赖人工。作者希望探索LLM是否能生成操作上逼真的ATC通信，以推进该领域的自动化辅助。

Method: 采用飞行中的人工转录作为地面真值（P0），通过飞行员在环过程设计五种约束递增的提示结构（P1-P5），嵌入有状态的多轮对话流水线（模型扮演ATC回应固定飞行员转录）。在九个开源与闭源LLM上变化三个因素：提示结构、是否提供另一实验飞行的转录作为上下文示例、模型是条件于自身先前回复还是注入地面真值历史。使用词汇、结构、语义相似度指标及经人类专家标注验证的GPT-5.5裁判进行评分。

Result: 提供上下文示例可提升相似度，但收紧提示不能提升性能；最轻量提示表现最佳，最重脚本化的提示因自身错误在对话中累积导致性能崩溃，而注入正确历史可以显著修复该问题。

Conclusion: 研究勾勒出LLM辅助ATC的具体可行路径及其当前局限性，指出对话历史的质量与提示复杂度之间存在关键权衡，为后续ATC自动化辅助研究提供了实验基础。

Abstract: Air traffic control (ATC) communication is a safety-critical dialogue that remains largely human-driven even as other parts of air traffic management have been semi-automated. In this article, we experimentally evaluate whether large language models (LLMs) can generate operationally realistic ATC transmissions. An experimental general-aviation flight flying over the San Francisco "Bay Tour" route is hand-transcribed and used as ground truth (P0). Through a pilot-in-the-loop process we design five prompt structures (P1-P5) of increasing constraint and embed them in a stateful multi-turn pipeline, where the model plays ATC to a fixed pilot transcript while conditioning on the accumulating dialogue history. Across nine open- and closed-source LLMs we vary the prompt, the presence of a worked transcript from a different experimental flight as an in-context example, and whether the model conditions on its own prior replies or on injected ground-truth history. Turns are scored with lexical, structural, and semantic similarity metrics and by an LLM-as-judge (GPT-5.5) validated against human expert annotation. Supplying a worked example improves similarity, but tightening the prompt does not: the lightest prompts perform best and the most heavily scripted one collapses as its own errors accumulate through the dialogue, which injecting correct history repairs. These results outline a concrete path and its current limits toward LLM-assisted ATC.

</details>


### [73] [Outcome Monitors: Recovery Affordances for Silent Tool Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19303)
*Sugam Panthi,Rabab Abdelfattah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Outcome Monitors，用于检测工具调用结果中违反"结果契约"（outcome contracts）的异常情况（如缓存错误页面或负面价格），并在违规时保留结果并发出声明违规属性及公共恢复工具的非约束性回执。在预设的ToolMaze和tau-bench评估中，该方法显著提升任务完成率（ToolMaze从10.9%升至28.1%），且消融实验表明恢复工具列表是有效成分，检测环节的扩展仍是未解决的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具调用超时时代理能看到失败并绕行，但缓存错误页面或负面价格等异常结果可能以预期格式返回并被代理当作事实消费，导致错误决策。现有方法缺少对这种"格式正确但语义违规"结果的检测机制。

Method: 引入Outcome Monitors，从任务不相交的轨迹中挖掘或从公共模式中推导结果契约，检测工具结果对这些契约的违反。检测到违规后，保留结果并生成非约束性回执，回执中指明被违反的属性及可用的公共恢复工具。

Result: 在注入故障的冻结预设定评估中，Outcome Monitors将跨四个模型、两个提供商家族的ToolMaze完成率从10.9%提升至28.1%，并在第三提供商家族中复现。tau-bench零售任务在两个层级上分别提升14.0和12.0个百分点。消融实验显示移除恢复工具列表会消除增益，恢复后增益恢复；诊断细节和时间无显著差异。在发布的事故分类法转录测试集上，词汇外检测率降至46%，但交付和完成率不变。

Conclusion: Outcome Monitors能有效检测并缓解"格式正确但语义错误"的工具结果，其增益集中在故障阻断完成的任务上，且恢复工具列表是回执中的关键活性成分。当前局限在于检测范围仍受限于结果契约词汇，超出该词汇的检测是未来工作方向。

Abstract: When a tool call times out, the agent sees the failure and can route around it. A cached error page or negative price can instead arrive in the expected format and be consumed as fact. We introduce Outcome Monitors, which detect violations of outcome contracts mined from task-disjoint traces or derived from public schemas. On a violation, the monitor preserves the result and issues a nonbinding receipt naming the violated property and public recovery tools. In frozen, prespecified evaluations with injected failures, Outcome Monitors raise ToolMaze completion from 10.9% to 28.1% across four models in two provider families and replicate in a third. In tau-bench retail, completion improves by 14.0 and 12.0 points on two tiers. In separate ToolMaze controls, removing the recovery-tool list eliminates the measured gain and restoring it recovers the effect; diagnostic detail and timing produce no detectable differences. Gains concentrate where the fault blocks completion. On a suite transcribed from a published incident taxonomy, detection outside the mined vocabulary falls to 46%, though delivery continues and completion is unchanged. Recovery tools are the active receipt content in these controls; extending detection beyond the contract vocabulary remains open.

</details>


### [74] [Beyond Imitation: Filtering On-Policy Distillation by Reasoning Progress](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19408)
*Chen Yang,Haiyuan Wan,Rengrong Xiong,Yize Chen,Danny H. K. Tsang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出R2-OPD方法，通过构建教师奖励与独立估计的推理进展奖励的双重轨迹内排序，在两者不一致时抑制蒸馏奖励，从而解决教师奖励与真实推理进展冲突的问题，提升推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的在线策略蒸馏（OPD）隐式假设教师奖励是推理进展的合适代理，但实际上教师奖励常与真实推理进展相冲突——推理步骤即使有明显进展，若偏离教师输出仍可能获得较低奖励。这种不匹配会误导策略优化。

Method: R2-OPD构建两个基于轨迹内的推理片段（reasoning span）排序：一个来自教师派生奖励，另一个来自独立估计的进展奖励。当两个排序不一致时，选择性抑制蒸馏奖励，从而减少与推理进展冲突的监督信号，同时保留有效的教师指导。

Result: 与标准OPD相比，R2-OPD在推理性能方面表现出一致的改进。

Conclusion: 通过奖励过滤机制纠正教师信号与推理进展之间的偏差，R2-OPD有效改善了在线策略蒸馏中教师反馈的质量，尤其在推理任务上取得了稳定提升。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) has emerged as an effective framework for post-training language models by pairing student-generated trajectories with dense token-level supervision from a teacher. However, OPD implicitly assumes that teacher-derived rewards are an appropriate proxy for reasoning progress, and therefore treats all teacher feedback equally during policy optimization. While in practice, this assumption does not always hold. We observe that teacher-derived rewards often conflict with genuine reasoning progress, as reasoning steps with clear reasoning advancement may still receive lower distillation rewards, simply due to deviation from teacher's outputs. To address this mismatch, we propose Reasoning-Progress-Aware Reward Filtering for On-Policy Distillation (R2-OPD), which constructs two within-trajectory rankings of reasoning spans, one from teacher-derived rewards and the other from independently estimated progress reward. Distillation rewards are selectively suppressed whenever the two rankings disagree, reducing supervision that conflicts with reasoning progress while preserving effective teacher guidance. Our approach shows consistent improvement over standard OPD especially regarding reasoning performances.

</details>


### [75] [Symposium: Trust via Auditable Records for Communities of AI Scientist Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19511)
*Dexter Pratt*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Symposium是一个形式化框架和实际实现，用于记录小型科学研究社区部署的AI智能体的操作。它提供长期、不可变的研究活动历史，保留分析、假设、数据和科学讨论的可审计轨迹，使智能体能够基于先前工作构建，并支持基于目的的可信度评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型科学研究社区在快速演化的环境中使用多种多样的AI系统，需要一个能够将科学社区的持久历史与操作这些历史的AI智能体分离开来的框架，以提供可审计的记录和可信度评估的基础。

Method: Symposium通过创建长期不可变的历史记录来捕捉科学论证，包括结构化声明、细粒度证据引用、假设和证据使用范围的明确声明。它采用分离架构，将社区的持久历史与AI智能体及操作系统分开，并提供发布基础设施、代理提示组件和文档的实际实现。

Result: 提供了一个可工作的实现，包括发布基础设施、agent提示组件和文档，使用户能够快速建立和运行自己的Symposium社区。

Conclusion: Symposium提供了一个与传统AI合著智能体或集成式AI研究环境不同的框架，专注于保存科学社区的持久历史和证据链，以支持AI辅助研究中的透明性和可信度评估。

Abstract: Symposium is a formal framework and practical implementation to record the operation of AI agents deployed by small scientific research communities. Symposium provides long-term, immutable histories of agent-driven research activity, leaving auditable trails of analyses, hypotheses, data, and scientific discourse. This shared record of published artifacts enables agents to build on prior work and preserves the evidence researchers and agents need to make purpose-dependent trust assessments. Symposium captures scientific argument, including structured claims, fine-grained evidence citations, assumptions, and explicit declarations of what material may and may not be used as evidence. Symposium differs from AI co-scientist agents or integrated AI research environments; it is a framework that separates a scientific community's durable history from the agents and other systems that operate on that history. It assumes that a community will use diverse AI systems in a rapidly evolving environment. A working implementation of the publication infrastructure, agent prompt components, and documentation are provided to enable users to rapidly set up and run their own Symposium community.

</details>


### [76] [From Retrieved Context to Runtime Control: Adaptive Compression for Edge-based RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19535)
*Zlatan Feric,Amir Taherin,Yanzhi Wang,David Kaeli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在边缘SoC上用于RAG系统的遥测感知自适应上下文压缩方法。通过在NVIDIA Jetson AGX Thor上对Llama和Qwen模型、Natural Questions和HotpotQA数据集、LLMLingua-2压缩器进行实验表征，发现生成阶段占RAG预算约90%的延迟和91%的GPU能耗。中间程度的压缩可在质量损失可忽略的情况下将GPU能耗降低53.2%、SoC能耗降低48.2%，而过度激进或过轻的压缩各有缺陷。论文主张通过设备遥测和工作负载特征动态管理压缩率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG虽能提升语言模型响应质量，但检索到的上下文会显著增加提示长度，带来prefill计算、KV缓存、内存流量、延迟和能耗的开销。现有上下文压缩方法要么使用固定预算，要么在离线阶段确定压缩率后再在推理时应用，这种静态视角无法适应工作负载变化和边缘设备实时状态。此外，在边缘SoC上压缩本身也要消耗延迟和能耗，可能抵消生成阶段的节省。

Method: 作者首先在NVIDIA Jetson AGX Thor边缘SoC上搭建实验平台，使用Llama和Qwen两种生成器、Natural Questions和HotpotQA两个数据集、LLMLingua-2压缩器进行系统化测量。实验分别表征了RAG各阶段（检索、压缩、生成）的延迟和能耗占比，并扫描不同压缩率下GPU能耗、SoC能耗和推理质量的变化，从而确定了自适应工作区间。

Result: 测量表明：对于7B-8B的大模型生成阶段占据主导，约占每次查询延迟的90%和GPU能耗的91%。压缩率的影响存在一个自适应操作区间：轻度压缩无法充分释放能耗节省机会，过度压缩则会损害推理质量。中等程度的压缩可在质量损失可忽略的前提下将GPU能耗降低最多53.2%、SoC能耗最多降低48.2%。

Conclusion: 论文主张应设计运行时策略，基于工作负载特征和边缘设备遥测数据动态管理压缩率，而非采用固定的或离线选定的压缩预算。这一方向为边缘RAG系统在能耗、延迟与质量之间的权衡提供了新的优化思路。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) improves language-model responses by grounding generation in external passages, which comes with overhead: retrieved context lengthens the prompt, increasing prefill work, KV-cache footprint, memory traffic, latency, and energy. Context compression offers a natural remedy by pruning retrieved text before generation. However, state-of-the-art context-compression methods are typically used with a fixed compression budget, or with the rate selected offline and then applied at inference time. This static view ignores both workload variation and the live state of the edge device. On an edge SoC, compression is not free: the compressor itself runs on the same SoC and consumes latency and energy that can offset any generation savings.
  This paper proposes a vision for telemetry-informed adaptive compression in edge RAG, grounded in experimental evidence. We characterize the compression tradeoff on the NVIDIA Jetson AGX Thor using Llama and Qwen generators, Natural Questions and HotpotQA datasets, and LLMLingua-2 compression. Our measurements show that generation dominates the RAG budget for larger models, reaching roughly 90% of per-query latency and 91% of GPU energy for 7B-8B generators. Exploring the impact of the compression rate reveals an adaptive operating region: mild compression can miss energy opportunities, and overly aggressive compression can hurt inference quality. Intermediate compression can reduce GPU energy by up to 53.2%, and SoC energy by up to 48.2%, with negligible quality loss. We argue for runtime policies that dynamically manage compression, guided by workload features and edge telemetry.

</details>


### [77] [Enforcing LLM Safety through DMD-based Classification of Prompt-Response Embedding Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19579)
*Mohamed Akrout,Olivera Kotevska,Dan Wilson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于Koopman动力系统框架的黑盒方法，用于对大语言模型生成内容进行安全分类。通过将提示词和响应投影到高维嵌入空间，分别拟合安全和危险两种状态下的Koopman预测模型，并利用差分残差分数对新输出进行分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM被广泛部署于高风险应用中，但其生成有毒、有害或违反政策内容的倾向构成重大风险。在黑盒场景下高效检测不安全输出仍然是一个开放性挑战，需要一种不依赖模型内部信息的安全检测方法。

Method: 将此前用于幻觉检测的动力系统框架扩展到LLM安全分类。具体步骤包括：(1) 将提示词和响应投影到高维嵌入空间；(2) 分别针对安全和不安全状态拟合基于Koopman算子的预测模型；(3) 引入差分残差分数，通过比较安全与不安全状态下的预测误差对输出进行分类。关键创新在于同时纳入提示词和响应的嵌入动力学，以捕捉两者之间的交互模式。

Result: 在三个安全基准和三个嵌入模型上的评估表明：融合提示词嵌入可带来一致的性能提升，尤其是在与因果解码器（如Llama-3）搭配时对交互依赖型违规的检测上表现突出；而响应纯违规场景则更受益于稠密语义嵌入表示。

Conclusion: 该研究开辟了用动力系统分析AI系统的新路径，而非传统意义上用AI来建模动力系统。实验结果验证了Koopman框架在LLM安全黑盒检测中的有效性，并为后续在不同嵌入模型与违规类型间的检测策略选择提供了指导。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in high-stakes applications, yet their tendency to generate toxic, harmful, or policy-violating content poses significant risks. Detecting these unsafe outputs efficiently in a black-box manner remains an open challenge. In this paper, we extend a recently proposed dynamical systems framework designed for hallucination detection to LLM safety classification. By projecting both prompts and responses into high-dimensional embedding spaces and fitting separate Koopman-based predictive models for safe and unsafe regimes, we classify new outputs using a new differential residual score that compares prediction errors of the safe and unsafe regimes. A key contribution is the incorporation of the prompt and response embedding dynamics, yielding fitted Koopman operators that capture crucial interaction patterns. We evaluate our black-box method across three safety benchmarks using three embedding models. Our results show that incorporating prompt embeddings yields consistent improvements, particularly for interaction-dependent violations when paired with causal decoders (e.g., in Llama-3), while response-only violations benefit more from dense semantic embedding representations. These findings opens the door for using dynamical systems to analyze AI systems rather than the dominant paradigm of using AI to model dynamical systems.

</details>


### [78] [Scientific Data Skills: Enabling Agent-Ready Scientific Data Services at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19625)
*Xiaohan Huang,Qingqing Long,Xiaolei Du,Siyu Pu,Jiawen Xu,Haotian Chen,Chenyang Zhao,Jinbiao Liu,Xuezhi Wang,Hao Wang,Hengshu Zhu,Yuanchun Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出科学数据技能（SciDSK），一种面向AI代理的数据集表示方法，将数据集特定知识与操作指南封装为可复用的代理技能，并构建了跨六个学科的科学数据技能库，实验表明该方法显著提升了代理驱动的数据集发现与解释能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据集表示主要为人类使用而设计，且科学数据分散在异构存储库中，无法为AI代理提供自主发现、解释和调用的充分支持，亟需一种面向代理的标准化数据集表示方案。

Method: 提出了SciDSK规范，整合数据集描述、科学背景、文件组织、使用流程、质量检查和溯源信息，同时将底层数据保留在原始存储库中；开发了系统化的构建流水线，基于权威数据集记录和配套材料进行grounding；建立了跨六个科学学科的科学数据技能库平台，支持包访问、持久标识和源数据集可追溯性。
